official Neo4j Community image without /var/lib/neo4j/products, flattened, config re-set) and the compose `neo4j` service on ke-neo4j.

store: Driver overlayfs, driver-type io.containerd.snapshotter.v1), Compose v5.1.4, Git Bash 5.3.9, GNU tar 1.35. Date 2026-10-23 (UTC), tester.
Why: a new Dockerfile and a compose change need a recorded Windows run before the unit commits (A34). Required by the orchestrator:
build.ps1 builds ke-neo4j:<tag>; launch.ps1 brings neo4j up healthy from ke-neo4j; in the live neo4j /var/lib/neo4j/products is absent and
no *fleet-management* file exists; docker image inspect config equals the official image's (N16 list); bundle.sh from Git Bash writes
images/ke-neo4j.tar and app/sboms/ke-neo4j.spdx.json with no neo4j.tar and no ke-vendor/neo4j; the file-tree comparison of both images with
the ownership diff on this containerd store (evidence, ruling 3); install.ps1 from that bundle loads ke-neo4j and neo4j comes up (NEW: the
U4 driver never ran install.ps1, so section 10 is added); teardown.ps1 cleans up; Windows-only tests and verify fast pass. Added mid-run by
the coordinator (review nits N4, N5): the ke-neo4j purl@version diff against U3's neo4j list, and both tar -tv listings saved for re-check.

Differences from the U4 r1 run, on purpose:
  - own project COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME=ke-tester-u4bwin and image tag KE_IMAGE_TAG=u4bwin-40e2181, built by build.ps1 in phase build from
    this tree (BuildKit cache, 8 s; ke-neo4j:u4bwin-40e2181 = sha256:deb601b3..., not any earlier ke-neo4j tag); removed in section 8 and,
    after install.ps1 re-loaded them, in the cleanup; the 'local' tag was never used or moved;

    final check). The install target got its own throwaway .env the same way and was deleted with the target;
  - NEW 3c (phase build): ke-neo4j config vs the official image, field by field over N16's list (13 fields incl. Os/Architecture), plus a
    whole-.Config compare without Labels; NEW 3c addendum (read-only): the ArgsEscaped value;

    phoenix 35, postgres 25, valkey 1, with the U4 r1 addendum's fixed sed pattern; NEW bundle-layout checks, a layer scan of
    images/ke-neo4j.tar (tools/tar_layers_u4b.py), the SBOM row diff against U4 r1's neo4j SBOM, the N4 TSV diff against
    tests/fixtures/sbom-u3/neo4j.pkgs.tsv, 3b-neo4j (offline_check.sh's ke-neo4j step with two negative controls) and 3b-tree (file tree
    and ownership diff; tools/tree_compare_u4b.py, which also compares size, and scripts/sbom_check.py tree; N5 listings saved);
    the bundle is kept for section 10 and deleted by the cleanup;
  - NEW 4d (phase up): the live neo4j image id, products/ absent, no *fleet-management* file, smoke.sh's own find probe compared as a
    whole string, ownership, the server uid (docker top), cypher-shell dbms.components();
  - NEW phase install (section 10) and Git Bash cleanup (described above);
  - tag restore: bundle.sh and install.ps1 both move ke-{app,gateway,neo4j}:bundle-40e21819680d onto this run's images. Unlike U4 r1's
    blanket restore, only tags that point at one of this run's three fresh image IDs (<scratch>/out-u4b/mine.txt) are moved back or
    removed, so a concurrent reviewer/security run would not be clobbered. Restored after 3b and after install; final check: as before.

==================== 3b-drift. U4 (ruling 6; PSC-14/22, N7, N10 B3): the image-side drift check against this run's ke-app SBOM, the way scripts/offline_check.sh runs it ====================

Commands are offline_check.sh's own (host half, after bundle.sh), with its variables set the same way; the one difference: the drift work dir is
made in <scratch> (mktemp -d <scratch>/.ke-drift.XXXXXXXX) rather than the repo's dist/, so this run writes nothing in the repo. The base pull
output is shown here (offline_check.sh sends its stdout to /dev/null). The SBOM run inherits COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME (ke-tester-u4bwin), as
offline_check's inherits ke-offline-<random> (ruling 7). The checker carries --label com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u4bwin-sbomcheck, as
offline_check.sh's carries ${project}-sbomcheck.

==================== Tester file: <scratch>/winproof-u4b.ps1 ====================
# U4b (PSC-26, Q67, N16: ke-neo4j) Windows proof driver (tester): the U4 rework r1 driver (docs/evidence/platform-sbom/u4/r1-windows-run.txt) with project, tag, marker
# and hash list (<scratch>\out-u4b\files.txt: A34 scope plus the U4b files) changed; NEW: 3c ke-neo4j config vs the official image (N16 list), 4d the live neo4j
# runs ke-neo4j with no products/ and no fleet-management file, section 8 also untags ke-neo4j, and phase 'install' (section 10: install.ps1 from the 3b bundle,
# launch.ps1 and teardown.ps1 in the installed target; the U4 driver did not exercise install.ps1). Section 3b (bundle.sh) runs from Git Bash between build and up.
# Own project and image tag so no other project or image is touched.
param([Parameter(Mandatory)][ValidateSet('pre', 'build', 'up', 'down', 'install', 'tests')][string]$Phase)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Continue'
$PSStyle.OutputRendering = 'PlainText'
$repo = 'C:\repos\Knowledge-engine'
$scratch = Split-Path -Parent $PSCommandPath
$shimDir = Join-Path $scratch 'shim'
$envBak = Join-Path $scratch 'env.bak'
$project = 'ke-tester-u4bwin'
$tag = 'u4bwin-40e2181'
Set-Location $repo
$env:COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME = $project
$env:KE_IMAGE_TAG = $tag
$filter = "label=com.docker.compose.project=$project"

function Section([string]$t) { ''; "==================== $t ===================="; '' }
function Step([string]$label, [scriptblock]$sb) {
    "PS> $label"
    $global:LASTEXITCODE = $null
    & $sb 2>&1 | ForEach-Object { "$_" }
    "[exit code: $global:LASTEXITCODE]"
    ''
}
function EnvDiff {
    Compare-Object (Get-Content .env.example) (Get-Content .env) |
        ForEach-Object { '{0} {1}' -f $(if ($_.SideIndicator -eq '=>') { '+ .env        ' } else { '- .env.example' }), $_.InputObject }
}
function ProjectPorts {
    docker ps --filter $filter --format '{{.Names}}  {{.Ports}}'
}

$pyMaps = 'import importlib.util as u, psycopg; psycopg.pq.version(); print("psycopg_c.pq:", u.find_spec("psycopg_c.pq").origin); print("libpq mapped:", sorted({l.split()[-1] for l in open("/proc/self/maps") if "libpq" in l})); print("psycopg_binary spec:", u.find_spec("psycopg_binary"))'
$pyPip = "import importlib.util as u; print(u.find_spec('pip'))"
$shLdd = 'for f in /opt/venv/lib/python3*/site-packages/psycopg_c/pq*.so; do echo "$f"; ldd "$f" | grep -E "libpq|psycopg_binary"; done'

        "compose projects named $project*: " + ((docker compose ls -a --filter "name=$project" --format json | ConvertFrom-Json) | ForEach-Object Name)
    }
}
'build' {
    Section '3. scripts\build.ps1'
    Step 'pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts\build.ps1' { pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts\build.ps1 }
    Step "docker image ls --filter reference=ke-*:$tag" { docker image ls --filter "reference=ke-*:$tag" }

    Section "3c. (PSC-26, N16; U4b): ke-neo4j:$tag (built by build.ps1 above) has the official image's config, N16's list; Labels recorded but excluded (ruling 5)"
    $neoBase = ([regex]::Match((Get-Content -Raw docker\neo4j.Dockerfile), '(?m)^FROM (\S+) AS runtime\r?$')).Groups[1].Value
    Step 'official base ref (docker/neo4j.Dockerfile, FROM ... AS runtime)' { $neoBase }
    Step 'official base in the image store (inspect only; build.ps1 does not pull it as a stack image)' { docker image inspect --format '{{.Id}} {{.Os}}/{{.Architecture}} {{len .RootFS.Layers}} rootfs layers' $neoBase }
    Step "docker image inspect ke-neo4j:$tag (id, os/arch, rootfs layers: flattened = 1)" { docker image inspect --format '{{.Id}} {{.Os}}/{{.Architecture}} {{len .RootFS.Layers}} rootfs layers; created {{.Created}}' "ke-neo4j:$tag" }
    Step "docker image history --no-trunc --format '{{.Size}} | {{.CreatedBy}}' ke-neo4j:$tag" { docker image history --no-trunc --format '{{.Size}} | {{.CreatedBy}}' "ke-neo4j:$tag" }
    Step 'per field (N16 list), raw docker JSON, ordinal compare' {
        $fields = @('Os', 'Architecture', 'Config.Entrypoint', 'Config.Cmd', 'Config.Env', 'Config.WorkingDir', 'Config.ExposedPorts', 'Config.Volumes', 'Config.User', 'Config.Healthcheck', 'Config.StopSignal', 'Config.OnBuild', 'Config.Shell')
        $bad = 0
        foreach ($f in $fields) {
            $o = (docker image inspect --format "{{json .$f}}" $neoBase) -join ''
            $k = (docker image inspect --format "{{json .$f}}" "ke-neo4j:$tag") -join ''
            $eq = [string]::Equals($o, $k, [StringComparison]::Ordinal)
            if (-not $eq) { $bad++ }
            '{0,-20} equal={1}  official={2}' -f $f, $eq, $o
            if (-not $eq) { '{0,-20}              ke-neo4j={1}' -f '', $k }
        }
        "fields compared: $($fields.Count); differences: $bad"
        "Labels (excluded, ruling 5): official=$((docker image inspect --format '{{json .Config.Labels}}' $neoBase) -join ''); ke-neo4j=$((docker image inspect --format '{{json .Config.Labels}}' "ke-neo4j:$tag") -join '')"
    }
    Step 'whole .Config without Labels (catches a field outside the list): ConvertFrom-Json, drop Labels, ConvertTo-Json -Compress, ordinal compare' {
        $o = ((docker image inspect --format '{{json .Config}}' $neoBase) -join '') | ConvertFrom-Json
        $k = ((docker image inspect --format '{{json .Config}}' "ke-neo4j:$tag") -join '') | ConvertFrom-Json
        $o.PSObject.Properties.Remove('Labels'); $k.PSObject.Properties.Remove('Labels')
        $oj = $o | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 10 -Compress; $kj = $k | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 10 -Compress
        "official keys: $((@($o.PSObject.Properties.Name)) -join ', ')"
        "ke-neo4j keys: $((@($k.PSObject.Properties.Name)) -join ', ')"
        "equal (ordinal): $([string]::Equals($oj, $kj, [StringComparison]::Ordinal))"
    }
}
'up' {
    Section '4. scripts\launch.ps1'
    Step 'diff .env.example .env' { EnvDiff }

    Step 'Invoke-WebRequest http://127.0.0.1:47100/healthz' { $r = Invoke-WebRequest -UseBasicParsing http://127.0.0.1:47100/healthz; "$($r.StatusCode) $($r.Content)" }
    Step 'curl.exe -sS -i http://127.0.0.1:47100/readyz' { curl.exe -sS -i http://127.0.0.1:47100/readyz }
    Step 'Invoke-WebRequest http://127.0.0.1:47101/ (Phoenix UI title)' { $r = Invoke-WebRequest -UseBasicParsing http://127.0.0.1:47101/; "$($r.StatusCode) " + ([regex]::Match($r.Content, '<title>[^<]*</title>').Value) }
    Step "docker port $project-gateway-1" { docker port "$project-gateway-1" }
    Step 'project containers and published ports' { ProjectPorts }
    Step 'Get-NetTCPConnection -State Listen -LocalPort 47100,47101 (host listeners)' { Get-NetTCPConnection -State Listen -LocalPort 47100, 47101 -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | ForEach-Object { '{0}:{1}' -f $_.LocalAddress, $_.LocalPort } | Sort-Object -Unique }
    Step 'TCP connect <this host non-loopback IPv4, not recorded>:47100 within 3 s (must be False)' {
        $ip = (Get-NetIPAddress -AddressFamily IPv4 | Where-Object { $_.IPAddress -notlike '127.*' -and $_.IPAddress -notlike '169.254.*' -and $_.InterfaceAlias -notlike 'vEthernet*' } | Select-Object -First 1).IPAddress
        $c = [Net.Sockets.TcpClient]::new()
        $ok = $false
        try { $ok = $c.ConnectAsync($ip, 47100).Wait(3000) -and $c.Connected } catch { $ok = $false } finally { $c.Dispose() }
        "connected: $ok"
    }

    Section "4b. (PSC-25/U0, repeated): psycopg implementation and libpq in the launched api (project $project)"
    Step "docker inspect $project-api-1 --format '{{.Config.Image}} {{.Image}}'" { docker inspect "$project-api-1" --format '{{.Config.Image}} {{.Image}}' }

    Step 'docker compose exec -T api python -I -c <psycopg_c.pq origin; libpq files mapped in /proc/self/maps; psycopg_binary spec>' { docker compose exec -T api python -I -c $pyMaps }
    Step 'docker compose exec -T api sh -c ''for f in /opt/venv/lib/python3*/site-packages/psycopg_c/pq*.so; do echo "$f"; ldd "$f" | grep -E "libpq|psycopg_binary"; done''' { docker compose exec -T api sh -c $shLdd }

    Step "docker compose exec -T worker python -c `"$pyImpl`" (worker shares ke-app)" { docker compose exec -T worker python -c $pyImpl }

    Section "4c. (PSC-14, N10a/U1, repeated): no pip in the launched api; flattened ke-app (project $project)"
    Step "docker compose exec -T api python -c `"$pyPip`" (must print None)" { docker compose exec -T api python -c $pyPip }
    Step "docker compose exec -T api /usr/local/bin/python3 -I -c `"$pyPip`" (must print None)" { docker compose exec -T api /usr/local/bin/python3 -I -c $pyPip }

    Step "docker compose exec -T worker python -c `"$pyPip`" (worker shares ke-app; must print None)" { docker compose exec -T worker python -c $pyPip }

    Section "4d. (PSC-26, Q67, N16; U4b): the live neo4j runs ke-neo4j:$tag; /var/lib/neo4j/products absent; no *fleet-management* file (project $project)"
    $neoFind = 'find / -xdev ( -path /var/lib/neo4j/products -o -name *fleet-management* -o -path /var/lib/neo4j/LICENSE.txt ) -print'

    }

    Step 'docker compose exec -T neo4j sh -c ''find / -xdev -name "*fleet-management*" -print | wc -l'' (must print 0)' { docker compose exec -T neo4j sh -c 'find / -xdev -name "*fleet-management*" -print | wc -l' }
    Step "smoke.sh's own probe: docker compose exec -T neo4j $neoFind (whole output must be exactly /var/lib/neo4j/LICENSE.txt, the control path)" {
        $out = (docker compose exec -T neo4j find / -xdev '(' -path /var/lib/neo4j/products -o -name '*fleet-management*' -o -path /var/lib/neo4j/LICENSE.txt ')' -print) -join "`n"
        $rc = $LASTEXITCODE

    }
    Step 'docker compose exec -T neo4j ls -la /var/lib/neo4j /var/lib/neo4j/plugins (ownership 7474; plugins empty)' { docker compose exec -T neo4j ls -la /var/lib/neo4j /var/lib/neo4j/plugins }
    Step "docker top $project-neo4j-1 (the server runs as uid 7474 after the entrypoint drops root)" { docker top "$project-neo4j-1" -o uid,pid,args | ForEach-Object { if ($_.Length -gt 160) { $_.Substring(0, 160) + ' ...' } else { $_ } } }
    Step 'docker compose exec -T neo4j sh -c ''cypher-shell -u neo4j -p "${NEO4J_AUTH#*/}" "CALL dbms.components() YIELD name, versions, edition RETURN name, versions, edition"''' { docker compose exec -T neo4j sh -c 'cypher-shell -u neo4j -p "${NEO4J_AUTH#*/}" "CALL dbms.components() YIELD name, versions, edition RETURN name, versions, edition"' }

    Section '5. Safety shim verification (before the refusal case)'
    $shimLog = Join-Path $scratch 'shim.log'
    if (Test-Path $shimLog) { Remove-Item $shimLog }

'install' {
    Section '10. (N16 A34, PSC-26; NEW, the U4 driver never ran install.ps1): scripts\install.ps1 from the 3b bundle loads ke-neo4j:bundle-<sha12> and writes the neo4j override; launch.ps1 in the installed target brings neo4j up healthy on it; teardown.ps1 -Volumes leaves nothing'
    $bundleDir = Join-Path $scratch 'bundle-u4bwin-amd64'
    $record = "$bundleDir.manifest.sha256"
    $instParent = Join-Path $scratch 'inst-u4bwin'
    $target = Join-Path $instParent 'ke'
    $instProject = 'ke-tester-u4bwin-inst'
    $sha12 = (git rev-parse HEAD).Substring(0, 12)
    Remove-Item Env:KE_IMAGE_TAG
    $env:COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME = $instProject
    $instFilter = "label=com.docker.compose.project=$instProject"
    $red = { param($s) ([string]$s) -replace [regex]::Escape($scratch), '<scratch>' }
    "COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME=$instProject (tester-owned, for the installed stack); KE_IMAGE_TAG unset (an operator's install uses the override, not a tag)"
    ''
    Step 'bundle (3b, kept for this section) and its release record: Test-Path; record content' { "bundle: $(Test-Path $bundleDir); record: $(Test-Path $record)"; & $red (Get-Content $record) }
    Step 'install parent and target do not exist yet (Test-Path)' { "parent $(& $red $instParent): $(Test-Path $instParent); target: $(Test-Path $target)" }
    Step "leftovers for project ${instProject}: containers, volumes, networks (all empty)" { docker ps -a --filter $instFilter -q; docker volume ls --filter $instFilter -q; docker network ls --filter $instFilter -q; 'end' }
    Step 'New-Item -ItemType Directory <parent>; owner and access (inherited from the user profile temp dir)' {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory $instParent | Out-Null
        $acl = Get-Acl $instParent
        "owner: $($acl.Owner)"
        $acl.Access | ForEach-Object { '{0} {1} {2} inherited={3}' -f $_.IdentityReference, $_.AccessControlType, $_.FileSystemRights, $_.IsInherited }
    }
    Step "ke-*:bundle-$sha12 tags before install (install loads and tags 'as'; restored by the Git Bash cleanup afterwards)" { docker image ls --no-trunc --format '{{.Repository}}:{{.Tag}} {{.ID}}' | Where-Object { $_ -match "^ke-[a-z0-9._-]+:bundle-$sha12 " } | Sort-Object }
    Step 'bundle.json images (ref, as, file)' { (Get-Content -Raw (Join-Path $bundleDir 'bundle.json') | ConvertFrom-Json).images | ForEach-Object { '{0} as {1} file {2} services {3}' -f $_.ref, $_.as, $_.file, ($_.services -join ',') } }
    Step 'Get-FileHash install.ps1 in the bundle vs its MANIFEST.sha256 line (quickstart step 3)' {
        $h = (Get-FileHash (Join-Path $bundleDir 'install.ps1') -Algorithm SHA256).Hash.ToLower()
        $line = Get-Content (Join-Path $bundleDir 'MANIFEST.sha256') | Where-Object { $_ -match '  install\.ps1$' }
        "Get-FileHash: $h"; "MANIFEST:     $line"; "equal: $($line -ceq "$h  install.ps1")"
    }
    $expect = ((Get-Content $record) -split '\s+')[0]
    Push-Location $bundleDir
    Step "cd <bundle>; pwsh -NoProfile -File .\install.ps1 -Target <target> -ExpectManifest $expect" { & pwsh -NoProfile -File .\install.ps1 -Target $target -ExpectManifest $expect | ForEach-Object { & $red $_ } }
    Pop-Location
    Step 'target top level (Get-ChildItem -Force -Name)' { Get-ChildItem -Force -Name $target }
    Step 'docker-compose.override.yml written by install.ps1' { Get-Content (Join-Path $target 'docker-compose.override.yml') }
    Step "docker image inspect ke-neo4j:bundle-$sha12 (loaded by install.ps1) vs the ke-neo4j:$tag id built in phase build (recorded by 3b in <scratch>\out-u4b\mine.txt; the tag itself went in section 8)" {
        $l = docker image inspect --format '{{.Id}}' "ke-neo4j:bundle-$sha12"
        docker image inspect --format '{{index .RepoTags 0}} {{.Id}} {{.Os}}/{{.Architecture}} {{len .RootFS.Layers}} rootfs layers' "ke-neo4j:bundle-$sha12"
        $b = ((Get-Content (Join-Path $scratch 'out-u4b\mine.txt')) | Where-Object { $_ -like "ke-neo4j:$tag *" }) -replace '^\S+ ', ''
        "built ke-neo4j:$tag id: $b; loaded id equal (ordinal): $([string]::Equals([string]$l, [string]$b, [StringComparison]::Ordinal))"
        "every tag on the loaded id: $((docker image ls --no-trunc --format '{{.Repository}}:{{.Tag}} {{.ID}}' | Where-Object { $_ -like "* $l" } | ForEach-Object { ($_ -split ' ')[0] }) -join ', ')"
    }
    Set-Location $target
    Step 'cd <target>; Copy-Item .env.example .env; Add-Content .env <marker> (throwaway, no real keys); diff' {
        Copy-Item .env.example .env
        Add-Content .env '# U4BWIN-THROWAWAY: tester copy of .env.example in the install target of the platform-sbom U4b Windows run; removed below'
        EnvDiff
    }
    Step 'host listeners on 47100/47101 before launch (empty = free)' { Get-NetTCPConnection -State Listen -LocalPort 47100, 47101 -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | ForEach-Object { '{0}:{1}' -f $_.LocalAddress, $_.LocalPort } | Sort-Object -Unique }
    Step 'pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts\launch.ps1 (in the target)' { pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts\launch.ps1 }
    Step 'docker compose ps --format json (service/image/state/health)' { docker compose ps --format json | ForEach-Object { $o = $_ | ConvertFrom-Json; '{0,-10} {1,-36} {2,-8} {3}' -f $o.Service, $o.Image, $o.State, $o.Health } }
    Step "docker inspect $instProject-neo4j-1 --format '{{.Config.Image}} {{.Image}} {{.State.Status}} {{.State.Health.Status}}' (must be ke-neo4j:bundle-$sha12, healthy)" { docker inspect "$instProject-neo4j-1" --format '{{.Config.Image}} {{.Image}} {{.State.Status}} {{.State.Health.Status}}' }
    Step 'Invoke-WebRequest http://127.0.0.1:47100/healthz; /readyz' { $r = Invoke-WebRequest -UseBasicParsing http://127.0.0.1:47100/healthz; "$($r.StatusCode) $($r.Content)"; curl.exe -sS http://127.0.0.1:47100/readyz; '' }
    Step 'docker compose exec -T neo4j sh -c ''ls -ld /var/lib/neo4j/products; echo "ls rc=$?"'' (must fail: No such file)' { docker compose exec -T neo4j sh -c 'ls -ld /var/lib/neo4j/products; echo "ls rc=$?"' }
    Step "smoke.sh's own probe in the installed neo4j (whole output must be exactly /var/lib/neo4j/LICENSE.txt)" {
        $out = (docker compose exec -T neo4j find / -xdev '(' -path /var/lib/neo4j/products -o -name '*fleet-management*' -o -path /var/lib/neo4j/LICENSE.txt ')' -print) -join "`n"
        $rc = $LASTEXITCODE
        "output: '$out'"
        "equals '/var/lib/neo4j/LICENSE.txt' (ordinal): $([string]::Equals($out, '/var/lib/neo4j/LICENSE.txt', [StringComparison]::Ordinal))"
        $global:LASTEXITCODE = $rc
    }
    Step 'cypher-shell in the installed neo4j: dbms.components()' { docker compose exec -T neo4j sh -c 'cypher-shell -u neo4j -p "${NEO4J_AUTH#*/}" "CALL dbms.components() YIELD name, versions, edition RETURN name, versions, edition"' }
    Step 'pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts\teardown.ps1 -Volumes (in the target)' { pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts\teardown.ps1 -Volumes }
    Step "after teardown: containers, networks, volumes, compose projects of $instProject (all empty)" {
        "containers: $(@(docker ps -a -q --filter $instFilter).Count)"
        "networks: $(@(docker network ls -q --filter $instFilter).Count)"
        "volumes: $(@(docker volume ls -q --filter $instFilter).Count) (by name ${instProject}_: $(@(docker volume ls -q --filter "name=${instProject}_").Count))"
        "compose projects: $(@(docker compose ls -a -q --filter "name=$instProject").Count)"
    }
    Set-Location $repo
    Step 'Remove-Item -Recurse -Force <parent> (target and its throwaway .env); Test-Path' { Remove-Item -Recurse -Force $instParent; "parent: $(Test-Path $instParent)" }
}

==================== Tester file: <scratch>/shim/docker.cmd ====================
@pwsh -NoProfile -File "%~dp0shim.ps1" %*
@exit /b %ERRORLEVEL%

==================== Tester file: <scratch>/shim/shim.ps1 ====================
# Safety shim (tester, U3 Windows proof; copied from the U2 shim): logs each docker call, refuses any `docker compose ... up` with exit 99, passes the rest to the real docker.exe.
if ($env:KE_SHIM_LOG) { Add-Content -Path $env:KE_SHIM_LOG -Value ($args -join ' ') }
if (($args -contains 'compose') -and ($args -contains 'up')) { Write-Output 'shim: intercepted docker compose up (exit 99)'; exit 99 }
& 'C:\Program Files\Docker\Docker\resources\bin\docker.exe' @args
exit $LASTEXITCODE

# tester wrapper: the winget jq.exe in binary mode (-b, LF output), its documented use under MSYS2/Git Bash
exec "<profile>/AppData/Local/Microsoft/WinGet/Packages/jqlang.jq_Microsoft.Winget.Source_8wekyb3d8bbwe/jq.exe" -b "$@"

==================== Tester file: <scratch>/bundle-u4b.sh ====================
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Section 3b (tester, U4b Windows proof, ke-neo4j): the U4 rework r1 section 3b script with project/tag/out dir changed, ke-neo4j in
# the image and tag lists, the expected unlicensed counts with ke-neo4j 27 (U4b GREEN) and the addendum's fixed sed pattern, and
# NEW: the bundle layout checks (images/ke-neo4j.tar, app/sboms/ke-neo4j.spdx.json; no neo4j.tar, no ke-vendor/neo4j), a layer
# scan of images/ke-neo4j.tar, the SBOM package diff against U4's neo4j SBOM, 3b-neo4j (offline_check.sh's ke-neo4j step, with
# two negative controls) and 3b-tree (file tree and ownership diff of both images on this containerd store; evidence, ruling 3).
# The bundle is kept for section 10 (install.ps1) and deleted by cleanup-u4b.sh. Tag restore only touches tags on this run's
# fresh image IDs. The U4 r1 script's own header follows.
# Section 3b (tester, U4 rework r1 Windows proof): the U4 section 3b script with project/tag/out dir changed, a Git Bash
# tmpfs probe on a created sbom container, the cache-warning count that must be 0, the expected unlicensed counts, and
# the drift checker's --label as offline_check.sh now passes it. The U4 script's own header follows.
# Section 3b (tester, U4 Windows proof): scripts/bundle.sh once from Git Bash on this host, to a scratch --out
# (the U3 section 3b script, project/tag changed), then the U4 additions: the per-image unlicensed report lines,
# the syft /tmp/cache warning, and the image-side drift check run the way scripts/offline_check.sh runs it
# (pull the base by digest, docker save, base SBOM through docker-compose.sbom.yml, sbom_check.py drift in ke-app).
set -u
S=<scratch>
R=/c/repos/Knowledge-engine
SM="$(cygpath -m "$S")"; SW="$(cygpath -w "$S")"
export RED_S="$S" RED_SM="$SM" RED_SW="$SW"
red() { perl -pe 's/\Q$ENV{RED_S}\E/<scratch>/gi; s/\Q$ENV{RED_SM}\E/<scratch>/gi; s/\Q$ENV{RED_SW}\E/<scratch>/gi; s#<profile>#<profile>#gi; s#<profile>#<profile>#gi; s#<profile>#<profile>#gi'; }
run() { echo "\$ $1" | red; bash -c "$1" 2>&1 | red; echo "[exit code: ${PIPESTATUS[0]}]"; echo; }
sec() { echo; echo "==================== $1 ===================="; echo; }
restore_tags() {  # $1: when. Only a tag that now points at one of this run's fresh image IDs is touched (the reviewer and security share this store).
  echo "--- bundle-tag restore ($1): tags on this run's fresh images go back to their earlier image (or go, if new)"
  docker image ls --no-trunc --format '{{.Repository}}:{{.Tag}} {{.ID}}' | grep -E "^ke-(app|gateway|neo4j):bundle-$SHA12 |^ke-vendor/" | sort > "$S/out-u4b/tags-now.txt"
  run "diff \"$S/out-u4b/tags-before.txt\" \"$S/out-u4b/tags-now.txt\" && echo 'bundle and ke-vendor tags: unchanged'"
  MINE=" $(cut -d' ' -f2 "$S/out-u4b/mine.txt" | tr '\n' ' ') "
  while read -r t id; do
    case "$MINE" in *" $id "*) ;; *) continue ;; esac
    before="$(awk -v t="$t" '$1 == t { print $2 }' "$S/out-u4b/tags-before.txt")"
    if [ -n "$before" ]; then
      [ "$before" = "$id" ] || run "docker tag $before $t  # back to its image before this run"
    else
      run "docker image rm $t  # a tag this run added, on a fresh tester image"
    fi
  done < "$S/out-u4b/tags-now.txt"
  run "docker image ls --no-trunc --format '{{.Repository}}:{{.Tag}} {{.ID}}' | grep -E '^ke-(app|gateway|neo4j):bundle-$SHA12 |^ke-vendor/' | sort | diff \"$S/out-u4b/tags-before.txt\" - && echo 'bundle and ke-vendor tags: as before section 3b'"
}
cd "$R"
export COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME=ke-tester-u4bwin KE_IMAGE_TAG=u4bwin-40e2181
export PATH="$S/bin:$PATH"
OUT="$S/bundle-u4bwin-amd64"
SHA12="$(git rev-parse HEAD | cut -c1-12)"
RAW="$S/out-u4b/03b-bundle-raw.txt"
project=ke-tester-u4bwin
export S R OUT RAW project

sec "3b. (PSC-13/U3, U4, U4 rework, repeated; U4b: ke-neo4j bundled as first-party, images/ke-neo4j.tar and app/sboms/ke-neo4j.spdx.json, no neo4j.tar, no ke-vendor/neo4j): scripts/bundle.sh once from Git Bash on this Windows host, to a scratch --out"
echo "date (UTC): $(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)"
echo "COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME=$COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME KE_IMAGE_TAG=$KE_IMAGE_TAG (exported, as build.ps1 tagged the images in phase build); --out <scratch>/bundle-u4bwin-amd64"
echo
run 'bash --version | head -1; uname -s -r; command -v cygpath docker'
echo '--- jq: bundle.sh needs it on PATH; the host has jq 1.8.2 from winget, not on the Git Bash PATH, and native jq.exe writes CRLF:'
run 'J=<profile>/AppData/Local/Microsoft/WinGet/Packages/jqlang.jq_Microsoft.Winget.Source_8wekyb3d8bbwe/jq.exe; "$J" --version; printf "%s" "{\"a\":\"b\"}" | "$J" -er .a | od -c | head -1; sha256sum "$J"'
echo '--- so <scratch>/bin/jq (on PATH for this section only) runs it with -b, LF output (as in U3):'
run 'cat "$(command -v jq)"; command -v jq; jq --version; printf "%s" "{\"a\":\"b\"}" | jq -er .a | od -c | head -1'
run 'echo "id -u:id -g = $(id -u):$(id -g) (the KE_SBOM_USER bundle.sh passes)"'

echo '--- fail-closed from Git Bash (U3 L-2, repeated): KE_SBOM_USER empty, and unset; then set, with XDG_CACHE_HOME in the resolved environment (U4)'
run 'KE_SBOM_IN=/nonexistent KE_SBOM_OUT=/nonexistent KE_SBOM_USER= docker compose --env-file .env.example -f docker-compose.sbom.yml config --images sbom'
run 'env -u KE_SBOM_USER KE_SBOM_IN=/nonexistent KE_SBOM_OUT=/nonexistent docker compose --env-file .env.example -f docker-compose.sbom.yml config --images sbom'
run 'KE_SBOM_IN=/nonexistent KE_SBOM_OUT=/nonexistent KE_SBOM_USER="$(id -u):$(id -g)" docker compose --env-file .env.example -f docker-compose.sbom.yml config --images sbom'
run 'KE_SBOM_IN=/nonexistent KE_SBOM_OUT=/nonexistent KE_SBOM_USER="$(id -u):$(id -g)" docker compose --env-file .env.example -f docker-compose.sbom.yml config --format json | jq -c ".services.sbom | {user, tmpfs, XDG_CACHE_HOME: .environment.XDG_CACHE_HOME, TMPDIR: .environment.TMPDIR}"'

echo '--- U4 rework (review B1) from Git Bash: the tmpfs options compose gives a real sbom container (created with --pull never, never started, then removed)'
run 'D="$S/tmpfs-probe-u4b"; mkdir -p -- "$D/in" "$D/out"; MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1 KE_SBOM_IN="$(cygpath -m "$D/in")" KE_SBOM_OUT="$(cygpath -m "$D/out")" KE_SBOM_USER="$(id -u):$(id -g)" docker compose -p ke-tester-u4bwin-diag --env-file .env.example -f docker-compose.sbom.yml create --pull never sbom; id=$(docker ps -a -q --filter label=com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u4bwin-diag); docker inspect --format "State={{.State.Status}} User={{.Config.User}} Tmpfs={{json .HostConfig.Tmpfs}} ReadonlyRootfs={{.HostConfig.ReadonlyRootfs}} NetworkMode={{.HostConfig.NetworkMode}}" "$id"; docker inspect --format "{{range .Config.Env}}{{println .}}{{end}}" "$id" | grep -E "^(XDG_CACHE_HOME|TMPDIR)="; MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1 KE_SBOM_IN="$(cygpath -m "$D/in")" KE_SBOM_OUT="$(cygpath -m "$D/out")" KE_SBOM_USER="$(id -u):$(id -g)" docker compose -p ke-tester-u4bwin-diag --env-file .env.example -f docker-compose.sbom.yml rm -f sbom; echo "left: $(docker ps -a -q --filter label=com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u4bwin-diag | wc -l)"; rm -rf -- "$D"'

echo '--- before'
run "ls -d \"$OUT\" \"$OUT.manifest.sha256\"  # must not exist"
run 'ls -d "${TMPDIR:-/tmp}"/ke-sbom.* 2>/dev/null | wc -l; ls -d "$S"/.ke-sbom.* "$S"/.ke-drift.* 2>/dev/null | wc -l  # work dirs before: the U3 location (TMPDIR), then the U4 one beside --out (review N6) plus drift work dirs'
run 'docker compose -f docker-compose.yml --env-file .env.example config --format json | jq -r ".services|to_entries[]|\"\(.key) \(.value.image)\"" | sort  # the refs bundle.sh will save'
run "docker image inspect --format '{{index .RepoTags 0}} {{.Os}}/{{.Architecture}} {{.Id}}' ke-app:u4bwin-40e2181 ke-gateway:u4bwin-40e2181 ke-neo4j:u4bwin-40e2181"
run "docker image ls --no-trunc --format '{{.Repository}}:{{.Tag}} {{.ID}}' | grep -E '^ke-(app|gateway|neo4j):bundle-$SHA12 |^ke-vendor/|^anchore/syft' | sort  # tags bundle.sh (re)points; restored after if moved"
docker image ls --no-trunc --format '{{.Repository}}:{{.Tag}} {{.ID}}' | grep -E "^ke-(app|gateway|neo4j):bundle-$SHA12 |^ke-vendor/" | sort > "$S/out-u4b/tags-before.txt"
run 'docker ps -a --format "{{.Names}} {{.Label \"com.docker.compose.project\"}}" | grep -c "ke-tester-u4bwin"  # containers of ke-tester-u4bwin* projects (expect 0: stack not up yet)'

echo '--- freshness (security M-1): bundle.sh uses KE_IMAGE_TAG=u4bwin-40e2181, the tag phase build just built from this tree'
run "docker image inspect --format '{{index .RepoTags 0}} id={{.Id}} created={{.Created}} {{.Os}}/{{.Architecture}}' ke-app:u4bwin-40e2181 ke-gateway:u4bwin-40e2181 ke-neo4j:u4bwin-40e2181"
APPID="$(docker image inspect -f "{{.Id}}" ke-app:u4bwin-40e2181)"; GWID="$(docker image inspect -f "{{.Id}}" ke-gateway:u4bwin-40e2181)"; NEOID="$(docker image inspect -f "{{.Id}}" ke-neo4j:u4bwin-40e2181)"
printf "%s\n" "ke-app:u4bwin-40e2181 $APPID" "ke-gateway:u4bwin-40e2181 $GWID" "ke-neo4j:u4bwin-40e2181 $NEOID" > "$S/out-u4b/mine.txt"
run 'cat "$S/out-u4b/mine.txt"  # the fresh image IDs of this run (the tag restore and section 10 use them)'
run "docker image ls --no-trunc --format '{{.Repository}}:{{.Tag}} {{.ID}}' | grep -E ' ($APPID|$GWID|$NEOID)\$'  # every tag on the two fresh image IDs"
run "MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1 docker run --rm --network none --pull never --label com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u4bwin-probe --entrypoint python ke-app:u4bwin-40e2181 -I -c \"import importlib.util as u; print('psycopg_binary spec:', u.find_spec('psycopg_binary')); print('pip spec:', u.find_spec('pip')); import psycopg; print('psycopg impl:', psycopg.pq.__impl__, psycopg.pq.version())\""
run "MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1 docker run --rm --network none --pull never --label com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u4bwin-probe --entrypoint sh ke-app:u4bwin-40e2181 -c 'dpkg-query -W libpq5; ls -d /opt/venv/lib/python3.12/site-packages/psycopg_binary* 2>&1 | head -3'"

echo '--- the run (full output; time in seconds after it; a raw copy is kept for the greps below)'
echo "\$ scripts/bundle.sh --out <scratch>/bundle-u4bwin-amd64"
t0=$(date +%s)
scripts/bundle.sh --out "$OUT" 2>&1 | red | tee "$RAW"
rc=${PIPESTATUS[0]}
echo "[exit code: $rc] [elapsed: $(( $(date +%s) - t0 )) s]"
echo

echo '--- U4: the per-image unlicensed report lines bundle.sh printed (sbom_check.py finish, stderr), in run order'
run 'grep "^sbom_check: " "$RAW"'
run 'grep -E "^sbom_check: [a-z0-9-]+: unlicensed packages" "$RAW"  # the six header lines'
echo '--- U4 rework (review B1): syft cache warnings in the bundle.sh run (XDG_CACHE_HOME=/tmp/cache on tmpfs /tmp:mode=1777); the first count must be 0'
run 'echo "unable to get filesystem cache: $(grep -c "unable to get filesystem cache" "$RAW")"; echo "WARN lines: $(grep -c "WARN" "$RAW")"; echo "lines naming /tmp/cache: $(grep -c "/tmp/cache" "$RAW")"; echo "lines naming /.cache/syft: $(grep -c "/.cache/syft" "$RAW")"; echo "permission denied: $(grep -ci "permission denied" "$RAW")"'
run 'grep "WARN" "$RAW" | sort | uniq -c  # every distinct WARN line (none expected)'
echo '--- U4b: the per-image unlicensed counts against the expected ones (ke-app 19, ke-gateway 0, ke-neo4j 27 (U4b GREEN; neo4j was 28), phoenix 35, postgres 25, valkey 1), with the fixed sed pattern of the U4 r1 addendum'
run 'printf "%s\n" "ke-app 19" "ke-gateway 0" "ke-neo4j 27" "phoenix 35" "postgres 25" "valkey 1" > "$S/out-u4b/expected-counts.txt"; grep -E "^sbom_check: [a-z0-9-]+: unlicensed packages" "$RAW" | sed -E "s/^sbom_check: ([a-z0-9-]+): unlicensed packages \(no licence, NOASSERTION or unidentified text\): ([0-9]+)( \(.*\))?$/\1 \2/" | sort > "$S/out-u4b/printed-counts.txt"; cat "$S/out-u4b/printed-counts.txt"; diff "$S/out-u4b/expected-counts.txt" "$S/out-u4b/printed-counts.txt" && echo "printed counts == expected"'

echo '--- the six SBOMs (and the image tars they describe)'
run "cd \"$OUT\" && ls -la app/sboms && stat -c '%s bytes  %n' app/sboms/* && stat -c '%s bytes  %n' images/*"
run "cd \"$OUT\" && cat bundle.json"
run "cd \"$OUT\" && grep -c '' MANIFEST.sha256 && grep 'app/sboms/' MANIFEST.sha256"
run "cd \"$OUT\" && sha256sum -c --quiet MANIFEST.sha256 && echo 'MANIFEST.sha256: every file verifies'"
run "cat \"$OUT.manifest.sha256\"; (cd \"$S\" && sha256sum bundle-u4bwin-amd64/MANIFEST.sha256)"
run "\"$R/.venv/Scripts/python.exe\" -I \"$(cygpath -m "$S/tools/check_sboms.py")\" \"$(cygpath -m "$OUT")\" \"$(cygpath -m "$R")\"  # validate() per file, the tests' equivalent (U3 helper, unchanged)"
run "\"$R/.venv/Scripts/python.exe\" -I \"$(cygpath -m "$S/tools/recount_unlicensed_u4b.py")\" \"$(cygpath -m "$R/scripts/sbom_check.py")\" \"$(cygpath -m "$OUT")\"  # host recount with sbom_check.unlicensed_report on the written files (must equal the printed counts)"

echo '--- U4b (PSC-26, N16): ke-neo4j is bundled first-party: images/ke-neo4j.tar and app/sboms/ke-neo4j.spdx.json present; images/neo4j.tar, app/sboms/neo4j.spdx.json and any ke-vendor/neo4j absent'
run "cd \"$OUT\" && ls images app/sboms && for f in images/ke-neo4j.tar app/sboms/ke-neo4j.spdx.json images/neo4j.tar app/sboms/neo4j.spdx.json; do if [ -e \"\$f\" ]; then echo \"present: \$f\"; else echo \"absent:  \$f\"; fi; done"
run "cd \"$OUT\" && echo \"ke-vendor/neo4j: bundle.json \$(grep -c 'ke-vendor/neo4j' bundle.json), MANIFEST.sha256 \$(grep -c 'ke-vendor/neo4j' MANIFEST.sha256), NOTICE.md \$(grep -c 'ke-vendor/neo4j' NOTICE.md 2>/dev/null)\"; echo 'MANIFEST lines naming neo4j:'; grep neo4j MANIFEST.sha256; echo 'bundle.json image serving neo4j:'; jq -c '.images[] | select(.services | index(\"neo4j\"))' bundle.json"
run "\"$R/.venv/Scripts/python.exe\" -I \"$(cygpath -m "$S/tools/tar_layers_u4b.py")\" \"$(cygpath -m "$OUT/images/ke-neo4j.tar")\"  # tester layer scan of the saved tar (independent of sbom_check.py): no products/, products whiteout or fleet-management entry"
run "\"$R/.venv/Scripts/python.exe\" -I \"$(cygpath -m "$S/tools/sbom_diff_u4b.py")\" \"$(cygpath -m "$S/u4r1win-sboms/neo4j.spdx.json")\" \"$(cygpath -m "$OUT/app/sboms/ke-neo4j.spdx.json")\"  # N16: package rows vs U4 r1's neo4j SBOM (sha256 7502ead9...3769, u4/r1-windows-run.txt): plugin rows go, nothing else"
echo '--- review N4: ke-neo4j package list (purl@version) of this bundle against U3 neo4j (tests/fixtures/sbom-u3/neo4j.pkgs.tsv, a byte copy of docs/evidence/platform-sbom/u3/sboms/neo4j.pkgs.tsv); the TSV is written by the U3 generator tools/sbom_evidence.py and saved as docs/evidence/platform-sbom/u4b/windows-ke-neo4j.pkgs.tsv'
run "cmp tests/fixtures/sbom-u3/neo4j.pkgs.tsv docs/evidence/platform-sbom/u3/sboms/neo4j.pkgs.tsv && echo 'fixture == u3 evidence copy'; sha256sum tests/fixtures/sbom-u3/neo4j.pkgs.tsv"
run "\"$R/.venv/Scripts/python.exe\" -I \"$(cygpath -m "$S/tools/sbom_evidence.py")\" tsv \"$(cygpath -m "$OUT/app/sboms/ke-neo4j.spdx.json")\" > docs/evidence/platform-sbom/u4b/windows-ke-neo4j.pkgs.tsv && sha256sum docs/evidence/platform-sbom/u4b/windows-ke-neo4j.pkgs.tsv && stat -c '%s bytes' docs/evidence/platform-sbom/u4b/windows-ke-neo4j.pkgs.tsv"
run "bash \"$S/tools/pkgs_diff_u4b.sh\" tests/fixtures/sbom-u3/neo4j.pkgs.tsv docs/evidence/platform-sbom/u4b/windows-ke-neo4j.pkgs.tsv"

sec "3b-drift. U4 (ruling 6; PSC-14/22, N7, N10 B3): the image-side drift check against this run's ke-app SBOM, the way scripts/offline_check.sh runs it"
echo "Commands are offline_check.sh's own (host half, after bundle.sh), with its variables set the same way; the one difference: the drift work dir is"
echo "made in <scratch> (mktemp -d <scratch>/.ke-drift.XXXXXXXX) rather than the repo's dist/, so this run writes nothing in the repo. The base pull"
echo "output is shown here (offline_check.sh sends its stdout to /dev/null). The SBOM run inherits COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME (ke-tester-u4bwin), as"
echo "offline_check's inherits ke-offline-<random> (ruling 7). The checker carries --label com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u4bwin-sbomcheck, as"
echo "offline_check.sh's carries \${project}-sbomcheck."
echo
run 'grep -n -E "^FROM .* AS runtime$|^ARG LIBPQ_VERSION=" docker/app.Dockerfile'
base_ref="$(sed -n 's/^FROM \([^ ]*\) AS runtime$/\1/p' docker/app.Dockerfile)"
libpq_pin="$(sed -n 's/^ARG LIBPQ_VERSION=//p' docker/app.Dockerfile)"
app_ref="$(sed -n 's/.*"ref": "\(ke-app:[^"]*\)".*/\1/p' "$OUT/bundle.json" | head -n 1)"
export base_ref libpq_pin app_ref
run '[[ "$base_ref" =~ ^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9._/-]*:[A-Za-z0-9._-]+@sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}$ ]] && echo "base_ref=$base_ref (passes the offline_check.sh pin regex)"; echo "libpq_pin=$libpq_pin"; echo "app_ref=$app_ref (from bundle.json)"'
echo '--- the base in the image store before the pull (present: an earlier offline_check run pulled it; not removed here, a shared image)'
run 'docker image ls --digests --no-trunc --format "{{.Repository}}:{{.Tag}} {{.Digest}} {{.ID}} {{.CreatedSince}}" python | grep "${base_ref##*@}"; docker image inspect --format "{{.Id}} {{.Os}}/{{.Architecture}}" "$base_ref"'
echo '--- pull by digest (offline_check: docker pull "$base_ref" > /dev/null || die ...; one call, no retry)'
run 't0=$(date +%s); docker pull "$base_ref"; rc=$?; echo "[pull elapsed: $(( $(date +%s) - t0 )) s]"; exit $rc'
DW="$(mktemp -d "$S/.ke-drift.XXXXXXXX")"; export DW
run 'echo "drift_work=$DW"; mkdir -- "$DW/in" "$DW/out" "$DW/out/tmp" && chmod 0700 -- "$DW/in" "$DW/out" "$DW/out/tmp" && ls -la "$DW" "$DW/out"'
run 't0=$(date +%s); docker save -o "$DW/in/base.tar" "$base_ref"; rc=$?; echo "[save elapsed: $(( $(date +%s) - t0 )) s]"; stat -c "%s bytes  %n" "$DW/in/base.tar"; exit $rc'
echo '--- the base SBOM (offline_check.sh lines, verbatim; hostpath = cygpath -m); its WARN lines are part of this output'
run '( hostpath() { if command -v cygpath > /dev/null 2>&1; then cygpath -m -- "$1"; else printf "%s" "$1"; fi; }; t0=$(date +%s); MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1 KE_SBOM_IN="$(hostpath "$DW/in")" KE_SBOM_OUT="$(hostpath "$DW/out")" KE_SBOM_USER="$(id -u):$(id -g)" docker compose --env-file .env.example -f docker-compose.sbom.yml run --rm --pull never sbom docker-archive:/in/base.tar --scope all-layers -o spdx-json=/out/raw.spdx.json >&2; rc=$?; echo "[sbom elapsed: $(( $(date +%s) - t0 )) s]"; exit $rc ) 2>&1 | tee "$S/out-u4b/03b-drift-sbom-raw.txt"; exit ${PIPESTATUS[0]}'
run 'echo "base SBOM run: unable to get filesystem cache: $(grep -c "unable to get filesystem cache" "$S/out-u4b/03b-drift-sbom-raw.txt"); WARN lines: $(grep -c "WARN" "$S/out-u4b/03b-drift-sbom-raw.txt")"'
run 'stat -c "%s bytes  %n" "$DW/out/raw.spdx.json"; jq -r "\"spdxVersion=\(.spdxVersion) name=\(.name) packages=\(.packages|length)\"" "$DW/out/raw.spdx.json"'
echo '--- sbom_check.py drift in the ke-app image (offline_check.sh lines, verbatim)'
DRIFT='hostpath() { if command -v cygpath > /dev/null 2>&1; then cygpath -m -- "$1"; else printf "%s" "$1"; fi; }; repo_host="$(hostpath "$PWD")"; MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1 docker run --rm --network none --read-only --cap-drop ALL --security-opt no-new-privileges --pull never --user "$(id -u):$(id -g)" --label "com.docker.compose.project=${project}-sbomcheck" -v "$repo_host/scripts/sbom_check.py:/opt/ke/sbom_check.py:ro" -v "$repo_host/tests/test_notice_ke_app.py:/opt/ke/added.py:ro" -v "$repo_host/licenses:/opt/ke/licenses:ro" -v "$(hostpath "$OUT/app/sboms"):/sboms:ro" -v "$(hostpath "$DW/out"):/base:ro" --entrypoint python "$app_ref" -I /opt/ke/sbom_check.py drift'
LIC='--licence /opt/ke/licenses/CPython-3.12-incorporated.txt --licence /opt/ke/licenses/CPython-3.12-HACL.txt --licence /opt/ke/licenses/CPython-3.12-KaRaMeL.txt'
run "$DRIFT --sbom /sboms/ke-app.spdx.json --base /base/raw.spdx.json --added-from /opt/ke/added.py --libpq \"\$libpq_pin\" --lock /app/uv.lock $LIC && echo \"offline: image-side drift check: the ke-app SBOM matches its base (\$base_ref)\""
echo '--- what the checker compared (tester, host side, sbom_check.py loaded by path; informational)'
run "\"$R/.venv/Scripts/python.exe\" -I \"$(cygpath -m "$S/tools/drift_inputs.py")\" \"$(cygpath -m "$R/scripts/sbom_check.py")\" \"$(cygpath -m "$OUT/app/sboms/ke-app.spdx.json")\" \"$(cygpath -m "$DW/out/raw.spdx.json")\" \"$(cygpath -m "$R/tests/test_notice_ke_app.py")\""
echo '--- negative controls (tester): the same docker run, one input wrong each; both must exit 1 naming the problem'
run "$DRIFT --sbom /sboms/ke-app.spdx.json --base /base/raw.spdx.json --added-from /opt/ke/added.py --libpq 15.18-0+deb12u1 --lock /app/uv.lock $LIC  # control 1: wrong libpq pin"
run "$DRIFT --sbom /sboms/ke-app.spdx.json --base /sboms/ke-app.spdx.json --added-from /opt/ke/added.py --libpq \"\$libpq_pin\" --lock /app/uv.lock $LIC  # control 2: the ke-app SBOM as its own base (adds no Debian package)"
echo '--- drift cleanup (offline_check: rm -rf -- "$drift_work")'
run 'rm -rf -- "$DW"; ls -d "$S"/.ke-drift.* 2>/dev/null | wc -l'

sec "3b-neo4j. U4b (PSC-26, Q67, N16; orchestrator ruling 2): the ke-neo4j check the way scripts/offline_check.sh runs it after drift: config equals the official image's; no products/ or fleet-management plugin in images/ke-neo4j.tar or app/sboms/ke-neo4j.spdx.json"
echo "Commands are offline_check.sh's own (host half, after drift), with its variables set the same way; differences: the work dir is in <scratch>"
echo "(mktemp -d <scratch>/.ke-neo4j.XXXXXXXX) rather than dist/, the bundle path is absolute, and the pull output is shown (offline_check sends it"
echo "to /dev/null). offline_check pulls the official image before bundle.sh; here the pull follows bundle.sh (the image was already present)."
echo
run 'grep -n -E "^FROM .* AS runtime$" docker/neo4j.Dockerfile; grep -c $'"'"'\r'"'"' docker/neo4j.Dockerfile  # CR count (a CRLF checkout would break offline_check.sh'"'"'s sed)'
neo4j_base="$(sed -n 's/^FROM \([^ ]*\) AS runtime$/\1/p' docker/neo4j.Dockerfile)"
neo4j_ref="$(sed -n 's/.*"ref": "\(ke-neo4j:[^"]*\)".*/\1/p' "$OUT/bundle.json" | head -n 1)"
export neo4j_base neo4j_ref
run '[[ "$neo4j_base" =~ ^neo4j:[A-Za-z0-9._-]+@sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}$ ]] && echo "neo4j_base=$neo4j_base (passes the offline_check.sh regex)"; echo "neo4j_ref=$neo4j_ref (from bundle.json)"'
echo '--- the official image in the store before the pull (an earlier run pulled it; shared, not removed here)'
run 'docker image inspect --format "{{.Id}} {{.Os}}/{{.Architecture}}" "$neo4j_base"'
echo '--- pull by digest (offline_check: docker pull "$neo4j_base" > /dev/null || die ...; one call, no retry)'
run 't0=$(date +%s); docker pull "$neo4j_base"; rc=$?; echo "[pull elapsed: $(( $(date +%s) - t0 )) s]"; exit $rc'
NW="$(mktemp -d "$S/.ke-neo4j.XXXXXXXX")"; export NW
run 'echo "neo4j_work=$NW"; docker image inspect --format "{{json .Config}}" "$neo4j_base" > "$NW/official-config.json" && docker image inspect --format "{{json .Config}}" "$neo4j_ref" > "$NW/config.json" && chmod -R a+rX -- "$NW" && ls -la "$NW" && echo "official: $(cat "$NW/official-config.json")" && echo "ke-neo4j: $(cat "$NW/config.json")"'
NEO='hostpath() { if command -v cygpath > /dev/null 2>&1; then cygpath -m -- "$1"; else printf "%s" "$1"; fi; }; repo_host="$(hostpath "$PWD")"; MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1 docker run --rm --network none --read-only --cap-drop ALL --security-opt no-new-privileges --pull never --user "$(id -u):$(id -g)" --label "com.docker.compose.project=${project}-sbomcheck" -v "$repo_host/scripts/sbom_check.py:/opt/ke/sbom_check.py:ro" -v "$(hostpath "$OUT/app/sboms"):/sboms:ro" -v "$(hostpath "$OUT/images"):/images:ro" -v "$(hostpath "$NW"):/neo4j:ro" --entrypoint python "$app_ref" -I /opt/ke/sbom_check.py neo4j'
echo '--- sbom_check.py neo4j in the ke-app image (offline_check.sh lines, verbatim apart from the paths)'
run "t0=\$(date +%s); $NEO --official-config /neo4j/official-config.json --config /neo4j/config.json --sbom /sboms/ke-neo4j.spdx.json --tar /images/ke-neo4j.tar && echo \"offline: ke-neo4j check: config equals \$neo4j_base; no products/ or fleet-management plugin in its tar or SBOM\"; rc=\$?; echo \"[check elapsed: \$(( \$(date +%s) - t0 )) s]\"; exit \$rc"
echo '--- negative controls (tester): one input wrong each; both must exit 1 naming the problem'
run 'jq -c ".User = \"7474\"" "$NW/config.json" > "$NW/config-user.json" && chmod a+r "$NW/config-user.json" && jq -c .User "$NW/config-user.json"'
run "$NEO --official-config /neo4j/official-config.json --config /neo4j/config-user.json --sbom /sboms/ke-neo4j.spdx.json --tar /images/ke-neo4j.tar  # control 1: User set to 7474 in the ke-neo4j config"
run 't0=$(date +%s); docker save -o "$NW/official.tar" "$neo4j_base"; rc=$?; chmod a+r "$NW/official.tar"; echo "[save elapsed: $(( $(date +%s) - t0 )) s]"; stat -c "%s bytes  %n" "$NW/official.tar"; exit $rc'
run "$NEO --official-config /neo4j/official-config.json --config /neo4j/config.json --sbom /sboms/ke-neo4j.spdx.json --tar /neo4j/official.tar  # control 2: the official image's saved tar (holds products/) in place of images/ke-neo4j.tar"
run 'rm -rf -- "$NW"; ls -d "$S"/.ke-neo4j.* 2>/dev/null | wc -l  # offline_check: rm -rf -- "$neo4j_work"'

sec "3b-tree. U4b (N16 *Confirm at GREEN*; orchestrator ruling 3: evidence, not a gate): the file tree of ke-neo4j against the official image, by path, with the ownership diff, on this host's containerd image store"
echo "Both images: docker create (never started; label com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u4bwin-tree) then docker export, compared by path for"
echo "type, mode (setuid/setgid/sticky included), uid, gid, size and link target by the tester helper tools/tree_compare_u4b.py, then by"
echo "scripts/sbom_check.py tree (the developer's check: no size). Allowed difference: var/lib/neo4j/products/ and its two files."
echo
run 'docker info --format "Driver={{.Driver}} DriverStatus={{json .DriverStatus}} ServerVersion={{.ServerVersion}} OS={{.OperatingSystem}}"'
T="$S/tree-u4b"; export T
run 'mkdir -- "$T" && df -h "$T" | tail -1'
run 'oc=$(docker create --pull never --label com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u4bwin-tree "$neo4j_base") && kc=$(docker create --pull never --label com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u4bwin-tree "$neo4j_ref") && echo "created (never started): official ${oc:0:12} from $neo4j_base, ke-neo4j ${kc:0:12} from $neo4j_ref" && docker inspect --format "{{.Name}} {{.State.Status}} {{.Image}}" "$oc" "$kc"; t0=$(date +%s); docker export -o "$T/off.exp.tar" "$oc" && docker export -o "$T/ke.exp.tar" "$kc"; rc=$?; echo "[export elapsed: $(( $(date +%s) - t0 )) s]"; docker rm "$oc" "$kc" > /dev/null; echo "containers left (ke-tester-u4bwin-tree): $(docker ps -a -q --filter label=com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u4bwin-tree | wc -l)"; stat -c "%s bytes  %n" "$T"/off.exp.tar "$T"/ke.exp.tar; exit $rc'
run '"$R/.venv/Scripts/python.exe" -I "$(cygpath -m "$S/tools/tree_compare_u4b.py")" "$(cygpath -m "$T/off.exp.tar")" "$(cygpath -m "$T/ke.exp.tar")"'
run '"$R/.venv/Scripts/python.exe" -I "$(cygpath -m "$R/scripts/sbom_check.py")" tree --official "$(cygpath -m "$T/off.exp.tar")" --image "$(cygpath -m "$T/ke.exp.tar")"'
echo '--- review N5: both tar -tv listings saved under docs/evidence/platform-sbom/u4b/ (sha256 below), with the exact diff commands'
run 'bash "$S/tools/tree_listing_diff_u4b.sh" "$T/off.exp.tar" "$T/ke.exp.tar" "$R/docs/evidence/platform-sbom/u4b"'
run 'rm -rf -- "$T"; ls -d "$T" 2>&1'

echo '--- after: nothing left by the run'
run 'docker ps -a --filter label=com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u4bwin-sbom -q | wc -l; docker ps -a --filter label=com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u4bwin-sbomcheck -q | wc -l; docker ps -a --filter label=com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u4bwin-notices -q | wc -l; docker ps -a --filter label=com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u4bwin -q | wc -l; docker ps -a --filter ancestor=ke-app:u4bwin-40e2181 -q | wc -l; docker ps -a --filter label=com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u4bwin-tree -q | wc -l; docker ps -a --filter ancestor=ke-neo4j:u4bwin-40e2181 -q | wc -l  # sbom, sbomcheck, notices; the drift SBOM run (project ke-tester-u4bwin); ancestor ke-app; the tree project; ancestor ke-neo4j'
run 'docker ps -a -q --filter label=com.docker.compose.service=sbom | wc -l  # sbom containers, any project'
run 'docker compose ls -a --filter name=ke-tester-u4bwin'
run 'docker network ls --filter name=ke-tester-u4bwin'
run 'ls -d "${TMPDIR:-/tmp}"/ke-sbom.* 2>/dev/null | wc -l; ls -d "$S"/.ke-sbom.* "$S"/.ke-drift.* "$S"/.ke-neo4j.* 2>/dev/null | wc -l  # work dirs left (trap removes; expect the counts from before)'
restore_tags "after bundle.sh"

echo '--- keep the six SBOMs and bundle.json in the tester scratch dir <scratch>/u4bwin-sboms; the scratch bundle and its record stay for section 10 (install.ps1) and go in cleanup-u4b.sh'
run "rm -rf \"$S/u4bwin-sboms\" && mkdir \"$S/u4bwin-sboms\" && cp -- \"$OUT\"/app/sboms/*.spdx.json \"$OUT/bundle.json\" \"$S/u4bwin-sboms/\" && (cd \"$S/u4bwin-sboms\" && sha256sum *.spdx.json)"
run "ls -d \"$OUT\" \"$OUT.manifest.sha256\"  # kept for section 10"
echo "[end 3b]"

==================== Tester file: <scratch>/cleanup-u4b.sh ====================
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Cleanup (tester, U4b Windows proof; NEW, Git Bash, after phase install): restores the bundle tags install.ps1 moved
# (it loads the bundle and tags each image 'as' ke-*:bundle-<sha12>), with the same rule as 3b (only tags on this run's fresh
# image IDs), then deletes the scratch bundle and its record. Functions copied from bundle-u4b.sh.
set -u
S=<scratch>
R=/c/repos/Knowledge-engine
SM="$(cygpath -m "$S")"; SW="$(cygpath -w "$S")"
export RED_S="$S" RED_SM="$SM" RED_SW="$SW"
red() { perl -pe 's/\Q$ENV{RED_S}\E/<scratch>/gi; s/\Q$ENV{RED_SM}\E/<scratch>/gi; s/\Q$ENV{RED_SW}\E/<scratch>/gi; s#<profile>#<profile>#gi; s#<profile>#<profile>#gi; s#<profile>#<profile>#gi'; }
run() { echo "\$ $1" | red; bash -c "$1" 2>&1 | red; echo "[exit code: ${PIPESTATUS[0]}]"; echo; }
sec() { echo; echo "==================== $1 ===================="; echo; }
restore_tags() {  # $1: when. Only a tag that now points at one of this run's fresh image IDs is touched (the reviewer and security share this store).
  echo "--- bundle-tag restore ($1): tags on this run's fresh images go back to their earlier image (or go, if new)"
  docker image ls --no-trunc --format '{{.Repository}}:{{.Tag}} {{.ID}}' | grep -E "^ke-(app|gateway|neo4j):bundle-$SHA12 |^ke-vendor/" | sort > "$S/out-u4b/tags-now.txt"
  run "diff \"$S/out-u4b/tags-before.txt\" \"$S/out-u4b/tags-now.txt\" && echo 'bundle and ke-vendor tags: unchanged'"
  MINE=" $(cut -d' ' -f2 "$S/out-u4b/mine.txt" | tr '\n' ' ') "
  while read -r t id; do
    case "$MINE" in *" $id "*) ;; *) continue ;; esac
    before="$(awk -v t="$t" '$1 == t { print $2 }' "$S/out-u4b/tags-before.txt")"
    if [ -n "$before" ]; then
      [ "$before" = "$id" ] || run "docker tag $before $t  # back to its image before this run"
    else
      run "docker image rm $t  # a tag this run added, on a fresh tester image"
    fi
  done < "$S/out-u4b/tags-now.txt"
  run "docker image ls --no-trunc --format '{{.Repository}}:{{.Tag}} {{.ID}}' | grep -E '^ke-(app|gateway|neo4j):bundle-$SHA12 |^ke-vendor/' | sort | diff \"$S/out-u4b/tags-before.txt\" - && echo 'bundle and ke-vendor tags: as before section 3b'"
}
cd "$R"
SHA12="$(git rev-parse HEAD | cut -c1-12)"
OUT="$S/bundle-u4bwin-amd64"
export S R OUT
sec "3b cleanup (Git Bash, after phase install): bundle tags moved by install.ps1 restored; the scratch bundle and its record deleted"
run 'cat "$S/out-u4b/mine.txt"; echo; cat "$S/out-u4b/tags-before.txt"  # fresh IDs; tags before 3b'
restore_tags "after install.ps1"
run "rm -rf -- \"$OUT\" \"$OUT.manifest.sha256\"; ls -d \"$OUT\" \"$OUT.manifest.sha256\""
echo '--- the fresh tester images, now untagged after the restore (install.ps1 re-created them by docker load): removed by ID only when no tag names them'
run 'for id in $(cut -d" " -f2 "$S/out-u4b/mine.txt"); do if docker image inspect "$id" > /dev/null 2>&1; then if docker image ls --no-trunc --format "{{.ID}} {{.Repository}}:{{.Tag}}" | grep "^$id " | grep -qv "<none>"; then echo "kept (tagged): $id"; else docker image rm "$id" && echo "removed: $id"; fi; else echo "absent: $id"; fi; done'
run 'docker image ls --no-trunc --format "{{.Repository}}:{{.Tag}} {{.ID}}" | grep -F -f <(cut -d" " -f2 "$S/out-u4b/mine.txt")  # tags left on this run'"'"'s fresh image IDs (expect none)'
echo '[end cleanup]'

==================== Tester file: <scratch>/final-check-u4b.sh ====================
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Final check (tester, U4b Windows proof): the U4 rework r1 final check with project/tag u4bwin, the 45 hashes (A34 scope plus
# the U4b files), ke-neo4j in the image and tag lists, and NEW: the install project, the install parent, the tree project and dir.
S=<scratch>
export S
cd /c/repos/Knowledge-engine
F="$(tr '\n' ' ' < "$S/out-u4b/files.txt")"  # 45 files: A34 scope plus the U4b files (files.txt)
c() { echo "\$ $1" | sed "s#$S#<scratch>#g"; bash -c "$1" 2>&1 | sed "s#$S#<scratch>#g"; }
c 'date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ'
c 'docker ps -a --format "{{.Label \"com.docker.compose.project\"}}" | grep -c "ke-tester-u4bwin"  # containers of any ke-tester-u4bwin* project: stack, -sbom, -sbomcheck, -notices, -probe'

c 'docker image ls --filter reference="ke-*:u4bwin-*"'

c 'docker ps -a -q --filter label=com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u4bwin-diag | wc -l  # the 3b tmpfs-probe project'

c 'KE_SBOM_IN=/nonexistent KE_SBOM_OUT=/nonexistent KE_SBOM_USER=1000:1000 docker compose --env-file .env.example -f docker-compose.sbom.yml config --format json | python -I -c "import json,sys; d=json.load(sys.stdin); s=d[\"services\"][\"sbom\"]; print(\"services.sbom keys:\", sorted(s)); print(\"user:\", s.get(\"user\")); print(\"tmpfs:\", s.get(\"tmpfs\")); print(\"environment.XDG_CACHE_HOME:\", s[\"environment\"].get(\"XDG_CACHE_HOME\")); print(\"environment.TMPDIR:\", s[\"environment\"].get(\"TMPDIR\")); print(\"top-level keys:\", sorted(d)); print(\"absent:\", {k: k not in s for k in (\"ports\",\"profiles\",\"networks\",\"privileged\",\"cap_add\",\"devices\",\"pid\",\"ipc\")})"'
c 'git status --short'

==================== Tester file: <scratch>/assemble-u4b.sh ====================
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Assembles docs/evidence/platform-sbom/u4b/windows-run.txt from the phase outputs (tester, U4b), with one literal-match
# redaction pass (scratch dir and profile path, every form) over the whole file, then a SID check.
set -u
S=<scratch>
O="$S/out-u4b"
DEST=/c/repos/Knowledge-engine/docs/evidence/platform-sbom/u4b/windows-run.txt
TMP="$O/assembled.txt"
hdr() { printf '\n\n#### %s ##########################################\n' "$1"; }
tf() { printf '\n==================== Tester file: <scratch>/%s ====================\n' "$1"; cat "$S/$1"; }
{
  cat "$O/header.txt"
  hdr "Git Bash, before phase pre (00-start)"; cat "$O/00-start.txt"
  hdr "PHASE pre"; cat "$O/01-pre.txt"
  hdr "PHASE build"; cat "$O/02-build.txt"
  hdr "SECTION 3c addendum (tester, Git Bash, run after phase tests while verify fast ran; read-only): the ArgsEscaped value and the two rootfs layers"; cat "$O/03c-addendum.txt"
  hdr "SECTION 3b (Git Bash, between phases build and up)"; cat "$O/03b.txt"
  hdr "PHASE up"; cat "$O/04-up.txt"
  hdr "PHASE down"; cat "$O/05-down.txt"
  hdr "PHASE install (NEW)"; cat "$O/06-install.txt"
  hdr "SECTION cleanup (Git Bash, after phase install; NEW)"; cat "$O/07-cleanup.txt"
  hdr "PHASE tests"; cat "$O/08-tests.txt"
  hdr "Final check (tester, Git Bash, after all phases and verify fast)"; cat "$O/10-final.txt"
  cat "$O/verdict.txt"
  tf winproof-u4b.ps1; tf shim/docker.cmd; tf shim/shim.ps1; tf bin/jq
  tf bundle-u4b.sh; tf start-u4b.sh; tf cleanup-u4b.sh; tf final-check-u4b.sh; tf assemble-u4b.sh; tf out-u4b/files.txt
  tf tools/recount_unlicensed_u4b.py; tf tools/tree_compare_u4b.py; tf tools/tar_layers_u4b.py; tf tools/sbom_diff_u4b.py
  tf tools/pkgs_diff_u4b.sh; tf tools/tree_listing_diff_u4b.sh; tf tools/sbom_evidence.py
  printf '\n==================== Tester files unchanged since U4 r1 / U3 (full text in docs/evidence/platform-sbom/u4/r1-windows-run.txt), and the derivation scripts (sha256 only) ====================\n'
  (cd "$S" && sha256sum tools/check_sboms.py tools/drift_inputs.py edit_u4bdriver.py edit_u4bscripts.py winproof-u4r1.ps1 bundle-u4r1.sh start-u4r1.sh final-check-u4r1.sh)
  printf '\n==================== $ COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME=ke-tester-u4bwin scripts/verify.sh fast (Git Bash, after the Windows phases; %s; full output) ====================\n' "$(cat "$O/09-verify-fast.rc")"
  cat "$O/09-verify-fast.txt"
} > "$TMP"
export RED_S="$S" RED_SM="$(cygpath -m "$S")" RED_SW="$(cygpath -w "$S")"
perl -pi -e 's/\Q$ENV{RED_S}\E/<scratch>/gi; s/\Q$ENV{RED_SM}\E/<scratch>/gi; s/\Q$ENV{RED_SW}\E/<scratch>/gi; s/\Q\<scratch>\E/<scratch>/gi; s#<profile>#<profile>#gi; s#<profile>#<profile>#gi; s#<profile>#<profile>#gi; s#C:\\\\Users\\\\adam#<profile>#gi' "$TMP"
B="$TMP.body"; cp "$TMP" "$B"  # the checks read a snapshot of the body, not the trailer being written
{
  printf '\n==================== Redaction check (tester, over this file before this trailer) ====================\n'
  printf 'literal "Users/adam" or "Users\\adam" (any case) left: %s\n' "$(grep -ciE 'users[/\\]+adam' "$B")"
  printf 'scratch path form AppData/Local/Temp/claude (any separator) left: %s\n' "$(grep -ciE 'AppData[/\\]+Local[/\\]+Temp[/\\]+claude' "$B")"
  printf 'user SID prefix S-1-5-21- lines: %s\n' "$(grep -c 'S-1-5-21-' "$B")"
  printf 'lines naming <scratch>: %s; <profile>: %s\n' "$(grep -c '<scratch>' "$B")" "$(grep -c '<profile>' "$B")"
  printf 'every matching line (line number: first 70 characters); each is a pattern or label of this check inside the copied assemble-u4b.sh, not a path or SID:\n'
  grep -niE 'users[/\\]+adam|AppData[/\\]+Local[/\\]+Temp[/\\]+claude|S-1-5-21-' "$B" | cut -c1-78 | sed 's/^/  /'
} >> "$TMP"
cp "$TMP" "$DEST"
wc -l "$DEST"; tail -6 "$DEST"

==================== Tester file: <scratch>/tools/recount_unlicensed_u4b.py ====================
"""Tester helper (U4b Windows proof): tools/recount_unlicensed.py (U4) with neo4j renamed ke-neo4j. Recounts the unlicensed
report of each SBOM a real bundle.sh run wrote, with scripts/sbom_check.py loaded by path. argv: <sbom_check.py> <bundle dir>."""
import importlib.util, json, pathlib, sys

spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("ke_sbom_check_under_test", sys.argv[1])
m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(m)
out = pathlib.Path(sys.argv[2])
for name in ("ke-app", "ke-gateway", "ke-neo4j", "phoenix", "postgres", "valkey"):
    doc = json.loads((out / "app" / "sboms" / f"{name}.spdx.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    r = m.unlicensed_report(doc)
    print(f"{name}: {r['count']} {dict(sorted(r['by_type'].items()))}")

==================== Tester file: <scratch>/tools/tree_compare_u4b.py ====================
"""Tester helper (U4b Windows proof; N16 "Confirm at GREEN", evidence not a gate): compares two `docker export` tars by path:
type, mode (incl. setuid/setgid/sticky), uid, gid, size and link target. Independent of scripts/sbom_check.py (which leaves
size out). The only difference allowed is var/lib/neo4j/products/ and what is under it; a hard link written out as a regular
file is recorded, not failed. Prints an ownership diff (uid:gid histograms of both trees, per-path owner changes) and N16's
key paths. argv: <official export tar> <ke-neo4j export tar>. Exit 1 on an unexpected difference."""
import collections, sys, tarfile

KINDS = {tarfile.REGTYPE: "file", tarfile.AREGTYPE: "file", tarfile.DIRTYPE: "dir", tarfile.SYMTYPE: "symlink",
         tarfile.LNKTYPE: "hardlink", tarfile.CHRTYPE: "char", tarfile.BLKTYPE: "block", tarfile.FIFOTYPE: "fifo"}


def listing(path):
    out = {}
    with tarfile.open(path, mode="r:*") as tar:
        for m in tar:
            rel = m.name[2:] if m.name.startswith("./") else m.name
            rel = rel.lstrip("/").rstrip("/") or "."
            out[rel] = (KINDS.get(m.type, "other"), m.mode & 0o7777, m.uid, m.gid, m.size, m.linkname)
    return out


def products(rel):
    return rel == "var/lib/neo4j/products" or rel.startswith("var/lib/neo4j/products/")


def fmt(e):
    return "missing" if e is None else f"{e[0]} {e[1]:04o} {e[2]}:{e[3]} size={e[4]}" + (f" -> {e[5]}" if e[5] else "")


off, ke = listing(sys.argv[1]), listing(sys.argv[2])
print(f"entries: official {len(off)}, ke-neo4j {len(ke)}")
only_off = sorted(set(off) - set(ke))
only_ke = sorted(set(ke) - set(off))
print(f"only in official ({len(only_off)}): {only_off}")
print(f"only in ke-neo4j ({len(only_ke)}): {only_ke}")
unexpected = [r for r in only_off if not products(r)] + only_ke
diffs, owner, hard_as_file = [], [], []
for rel in sorted(set(off) & set(ke)):
    a, b = off[rel], ke[rel]
    if a == b:
        continue
    if a[0] == "hardlink" and b[0] == "file" and a[1:4] == b[1:4]:
        hard_as_file.append(rel)
        continue
    diffs.append(rel)
    if a[2:4] != b[2:4]:
        owner.append(rel)
print(f"attribute differences on common paths (type, mode, uid, gid, size, link target): {len(diffs)}")
for rel in diffs[:20]:
    print(f"  {rel}: official {fmt(off[rel])} | ke-neo4j {fmt(ke[rel])}")
print(f"hard links written out as regular files (recorded, not failed): {len(hard_as_file)} {hard_as_file[:5]}")
print("ownership diff:")
print(f"  common paths whose uid:gid differ: {len(owner)}")
for name, tree in (("official", off), ("ke-neo4j", ke)):
    h = collections.Counter(f"{e[2]}:{e[3]}" for r, e in tree.items() if not products(r))
    print(f"  {name} uid:gid histogram (outside products/): {dict(sorted(h.items()))}")
print(f"  products/ in official: " + "; ".join(f"{r} {fmt(off[r])}" for r in only_off if products(r)))
print("N16 key paths (official | ke-neo4j):")
for rel in ("var/lib/neo4j", "var/lib/neo4j/bin", "var/lib/neo4j/lib", "var/lib/neo4j/plugins", "data", "logs",
            "var/lib/neo4j/data", "var/lib/neo4j/logs", "startup", "startup/docker-entrypoint.sh", "opt/java/openjdk",
            "tmp", "var/tmp", "run/lock", "var/lib/neo4j/LICENSE.txt"):
    print(f"  /{rel}: {fmt(off.get(rel))} | {fmt(ke.get(rel))}")
for label, test in (("setuid", 0o4000), ("setgid", 0o2000)):
    a = sorted(r for r, e in off.items() if e[1] & test and e[0] != "dir")
    b = sorted(r for r, e in ke.items() if e[1] & test and e[0] != "dir")
    print(f"  {label} files: official {len(a)}, ke-neo4j {len(b)}, same set: {a == b}")
    for r in a:
        print(f"    /{r}: {fmt(off[r])} | {fmt(ke.get(r))}")
hl = sorted(r for r, e in off.items() if e[0] == "hardlink")
print(f"  hard links in official: {[(r, off[r][5]) for r in hl]}; in ke-neo4j: {[(r, ke[r][0], ke[r][5]) for r in hl if r in ke]}")
bad = len(unexpected) + len(diffs)
print(f"unexpected paths: {unexpected}")
print("RESULT:", "PASS (ke-neo4j == official minus var/lib/neo4j/products)" if bad == 0 else f"FAIL ({bad} difference(s))")
sys.exit(1 if bad else 0)

==================== Tester file: <scratch>/tools/tar_layers_u4b.py ====================
"""Tester helper (U4b Windows proof): opens a `docker save` tar (as bundle.sh writes images/<name>.tar), opens every member
that is itself a tar (the layer blobs), and reports per layer the entry count and any entry naming var/lib/neo4j/products,
a whiteout of it, or a *fleet-management* file. Independent of scripts/sbom_check.py. argv: <image tar>. Exit 1 on a match."""
import io, sys, tarfile

hits, layers = [], 0
with tarfile.open(sys.argv[1], mode="r:") as outer:
    for m in outer:
        if not m.isfile():
            continue
        f = outer.extractfile(m)
        head = f.read(512)
        if head[:2] == b"\x1f\x8b":
            mode = "r:gz"
        elif len(head) == 512 and head[257:262] == b"ustar":
            mode = "r:"
        else:
            continue  # json, index, config: not a layer tar
        f.seek(0)
        layers += 1
        n = 0
        with tarfile.open(fileobj=f, mode=mode) as layer:
            for e in layer:
                n += 1
                name = e.name.lower()
                if "var/lib/neo4j/products" in name or ".wh.products" in name or "fleet-management" in name:
                    hits.append((m.name, e.name))
        print(f"layer {m.name} ({mode}): {n} entries")
print(f"layer tars: {layers}; entries naming products/, a products whiteout or fleet-management: {len(hits)}")
for h in hits[:20]:
    print("  ", h)
sys.exit(1 if hits or layers == 0 else 0)

==================== Tester file: <scratch>/tools/sbom_diff_u4b.py ====================
"""Tester helper (U4b Windows proof; N16 "GREEN evidence diffs its package list against U4's neo4j SBOM: the plugin rows
go, and nothing else changes"): compares the package lists (name, versionInfo, purl) of two SPDX SBOMs and reports any package
or file entry whose name, sourceInfo or purl mentions var/lib/neo4j/products, fleet or genai.
argv: <old SBOM (U4 neo4j)> <new SBOM (U4b ke-neo4j)>."""
import collections, json, sys


def load(p):
    return json.load(open(p, encoding="utf-8"))


def key(p):
    purl = next((r.get("referenceLocator", "") for r in p.get("externalRefs") or [] if r.get("referenceType") == "purl"), "")
    return (p.get("name", ""), p.get("versionInfo", ""), purl)


def marks(doc):
    out = []
    for p in doc.get("packages") or []:
        blob = " ".join([p.get("name", ""), p.get("sourceInfo", "") or ""] + [r.get("referenceLocator", "") for r in p.get("externalRefs") or []]).lower()
        if "var/lib/neo4j/products" in blob or "fleet" in blob or "genai" in blob:
            out.append(("package", p.get("name"), p.get("versionInfo"), (p.get("sourceInfo") or "")[:120]))
    for f in doc.get("files") or []:
        n = (f.get("fileName") or "").lower()
        if "var/lib/neo4j/products" in n or "fleet" in n or "genai" in n:
            out.append(("file", f.get("fileName")))
    return out


old, new = load(sys.argv[1]), load(sys.argv[2])
a = collections.Counter(key(p) for p in old.get("packages") or [])
b = collections.Counter(key(p) for p in new.get("packages") or [])
print(f"old {old.get('name')}: packages {sum(a.values())}, files {len(old.get('files') or [])}")
print(f"new {new.get('name')}: packages {sum(b.values())}, files {len(new.get('files') or [])}")
gone, added = a - b, b - a
print(f"package rows only in old ({sum(gone.values())}):")
for k in sorted(gone):
    print("  -", k, "x", gone[k])
print(f"package rows only in new ({sum(added.values())}):")
for k in sorted(added):
    print("  +", k, "x", added[k])
mo, mn = marks(old), marks(new)
print(f"old entries naming products/, fleet or genai: {len(mo)}")
for m in mo[:20]:
    print("  ", m)
print(f"new entries naming products/, fleet or genai: {len(mn)}")
for m in mn[:20]:
    print("  ", m)

==================== Tester file: <scratch>/tools/pkgs_diff_u4b.sh ====================
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Tester helper (U4b Windows proof; review nit N4): the ke-neo4j package list of this run's bundle against U3's neo4j list.
# argv: <U3 neo4j.pkgs.tsv> <this run's ke-neo4j.pkgs.tsv>. TSV rows: purl ('-' when none), versionInfo, declared|concluded
# (tools/sbom_evidence.py tsv, the U3 generator). Compared as sorted multisets, so document order does not matter.
set -u
old="$1"; new="$2"
echo "rows: U3 neo4j $(grep -c '' "$old"); ke-neo4j $(grep -c '' "$new")"
echo "--- diff A: purl@version only:  diff <(cut -f1 OLD | sort) <(cut -f1 NEW | sort)   (the purl carries @version; field 2 repeats it)"
diff <(cut -f1 "$old" | sort) <(cut -f1 "$new" | sort); echo "[diff A rc=$?]"
echo "--- diff A2: purl and versionInfo:  diff <(cut -f1,2 OLD | sort) <(cut -f1,2 NEW | sort)"
diff <(cut -f1,2 "$old" | sort) <(cut -f1,2 "$new" | sort); echo "[diff A2 rc=$?]"
echo "--- diff B: whole rows, licences included:  diff <(sort OLD) <(sort NEW)"
diff <(sort "$old") <(sort "$new"); echo "[diff B rc=$?]"

==================== Tester file: <scratch>/tools/tree_listing_diff_u4b.sh ====================
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Tester helper (U4b Windows proof; review nit N5): writes the GNU tar -tv listings (numeric owners) of both docker export
# tars into the evidence dir and diffs them, so the file-tree result can be re-checked from the repo alone.
# argv: <official export tar> <ke-neo4j export tar> <evidence dir>.
set -u
off="$1"; ke="$2"; E="$3"
A=windows-tree-official.tvf.txt; B=windows-tree-ke-neo4j.tvf.txt
tar --numeric-owner -tvf "$off" > "$E/$A" || exit 1
tar --numeric-owner -tvf "$ke" > "$E/$B" || exit 1
cd "$E" || exit 1
tar --version | head -1
echo "export tars:"; sha256sum "$off" "$ke" | sed -E 's#  .*/#  #'
echo "listings (columns: mode uid/gid size date time path [-> target | link to target]):"
wc -l "$A" "$B"; stat -c '%s bytes  %n' "$A" "$B"; sha256sum "$A" "$B"
echo "--- diff 1 (exact, in tar order):  cd docs/evidence/platform-sbom/u4b && diff $A $B"
diff "$A" "$B"; echo "[diff 1 rc=$?]"
echo "--- diff 2 (date and time dropped, sorted):  diff <(awk '{\$4=\$5=\"\"; print}' $A | sort) <(awk '{\$4=\$5=\"\"; print}' $B | sort)"
diff <(awk '{$4=$5=""; print}' "$A" | sort) <(awk '{$4=$5=""; print}' "$B" | sort); echo "[diff 2 rc=$?]"

==================== Tester file: <scratch>/tools/sbom_evidence.py ====================
"""Tester helper: writes <image>.pkgs.tsv (purl, version, declared|concluded; document order; '-' when no purl)
from an SBOM, and the gpl-family-packages.md body from the TSVs. Subcommands: tsv <spdx.json> | md <tsv dir> [header line...]"""
import json, pathlib, re, sys

NOLIC = {"", "NOASSERTION", "NONE"}

def tsv(path):
    doc = json.loads(pathlib.Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    out = []
    for p in doc.get("packages") or []:
        purl = next((r.get("referenceLocator") for r in p.get("externalRefs") or [] if r.get("referenceType") == "purl"), None) or "-"
        out.append(f"{purl}\t{p.get('versionInfo', '')}\t{p.get('licenseDeclared', '')}|{p.get('licenseConcluded', '')}")
    return "\n".join(out) + "\n"

def rows(tsv_path):
    for line in tsv_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
        purl, version, lic = line.split("\t")
        declared, concluded = lic.split("|", 1)
        yield purl, version, declared, concluded

def classify(declared, concluded, mode):
    gpl = "GPL" in declared or "GPL" in concluded
    if mode == "both":
        nolic = declared in NOLIC and concluded in NOLIC
    else:
        nolic = declared in NOLIC or concluded in NOLIC
    return gpl, nolic

def table(items):
    return ["| purl | version | declared / concluded |", "|---|---|---|"] + [f"| {p} | {v} | {d} / {c} |" for p, v, d, c in items]

def md(d, mode="both"):
    d = pathlib.Path(d)
    lines = []
    stats = {}
    for t in sorted(d.glob("*.pkgs.tsv")):
        name = t.name[: -len(".pkgs.tsv")]
        all_rows = list(rows(t))
        gpl = [r for r in all_rows if classify(r[2], r[3], mode)[0]]
        nol = [r for r in all_rows if classify(r[2], r[3], mode)[1] and not classify(r[2], r[3], mode)[0]]
        stats[name] = (len(all_rows), len(gpl), len(nol), all_rows, gpl, nol)
        lines += [f"## {name}: {len(all_rows)} packages, GPL-family {len(gpl)}, no licence/NOASSERTION {len(nol)}", "",
                  f"### GPL-family ({len(gpl)})", ""] + table(gpl) + ["", f"### No licence / NOASSERTION ({len(nol)})", ""] + table(nol) + [""]
    return lines, stats

if __name__ == "__main__":
    if sys.argv[1] == "tsv":
        sys.stdout.write(tsv(sys.argv[2]))
    elif sys.argv[1] == "md":
        lines, _ = md(sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3] if len(sys.argv) > 3 else "both")
        sys.stdout.write("\n".join(lines))
